Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The waves variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 949 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 283 and intersection form of rank 3.

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_949(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 949. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^283(S, F) is trivial. QED.
